fixtures/prefix_detect.json {"0xff", "0b1010", "0o777"} → {value, base}

12.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug
Zero-padding error in D&C digits off by powers of base lo not zero-padded to half_len digits compare digit count per recursion level
Off-by-one in split point asymmetric recursion, slow convergence half = len / 2 vs ceil test on odd-length inputs; measure recursion depth
schoolbook accumulate overflow wrong parse for long strings near threshold intermediate BigUint not grown before add-mul-1 grow limbs before each accumulate; property test
base^chunk cache stale wrong conversion after modulus change mutable cache keyed incorrectly key cache on (base, chunk_size) tuple
Display of zero emits empty string "" instead of "0" digit collection skips empty vector explicit if digits.is_empty() push('0')
Hex case mismatch LowerHex emitting uppercase char mapping table error unit test format!("{:x}", x) == expected_lower

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • D&C to_string_base: O(M(n) log n) vs schoolbook O(n^2); crossover ~32 limbs measured empirically; expose CONVERT_SCHOOLBOOK_THRESHOLD as a tunable.
  • pow_table construction is O(log n) squarings via bn-mul; precompute once per (x_size, base) and reuse within a single to_string call.
  • Digit Vec<u32> → String assembly: preallocate capacity = digit_count to avoid realloc.
  • Parse D&C: split at mid-char, compute base^half_len once per recursion level, not per call.
  • Avoid BigUint clone in accumulate inner loop; use in-place add_mul_1 from bn-add.

12.8 Edge Cases

  • x = 0 → "0" in all bases.
  • Base 2: very long strings (1 char per bit); D&C engaged for > threshold; no allocation per bit.
  • Trailing zeros in string: "007" base 10 → 7; leading zeros stripped by parser.
  • base = 1 → Err(InvalidBase); base > 36 → Err(InvalidBase).
  • Max u64 + 1 → single 2-limb value; parse and format exact.
  • Empty string "" → ParseError::EmptyInput.
  • Negative prefix "-" for BigUint parse → ParseError; handled at BigInt layer.

12.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
D&C zero-padding bug roundtrip mismatch for specific sizes all large-number formatting broken disable D&C (schoolbook only) temporarily; bisect threshold
Parse panics on long input stack overflow in deep recursion unbounded recursion without base case bound recursion depth; convert to iterative loop
pow_table miscomputed wrong large-number format silent wrong output for > threshold cross-check pow_table[i] == base^(2^i) in debug build
from_str accepts invalid UTF-8 silent wrong parse malformed input produces valid-looking number validate byte-by-byte before digit mapping

12.10 Operational Notes

  • BIGNUM_TRACE_CONVERT=1: logs (x_limbs, base, method, pow_table_depth) per conversion call (debug build).
  • Benchmark: cargo bench --bench convert_bench for base-10 format/parse at 100, 1000, 10000, 100000 digits.
  • CONVERT_SCHOOLBOOK_THRESHOLD configurable via bn-convert build feature for experimentation.
  • FromStr impl for BigInt handles leading - sign; delegates to BigUint parse for magnitude.
  • Display and FromStr roundtrip tested in CI via fixtures/roundtrip_bases.json as a regression gate.

Phase 13 — exact rationals + public API surface

13.1 Objectives

  • Implement BigRational in bn-rational: construct, reduce, arithmetic, comparison, decimal output with rounding.
  • Enforce I10: gcd(num,den)=1, den>0, sign on numerator, NoSign iff zero.
  • Finalize public API across bn-uint, bn-int, bn-rational: operators (Add/Sub/Mul/Div/Rem/Neg), From/TryFrom, Display/Debug, Hash, Ord/PartialOrd.
  • Expose Montgomery, Barrett, modpow through a clean bn-mod public surface.
  • No change to limb kernel internals; this phase is purely the top layer and API finalization.

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

bn-rational/src/lib.rs — BigRational struct, construction:

  • BigRational::new(num: BigInt, den: BigInt) — compute g = gcd(|num|, |den|), divide both, move sign to numerator, assert den > 0 post-canonicalization; return DivByZero if den == 0.
  • BigRational::new_unchecked — internal only; skips gcd; used after arithmetic where caller guarantees canon state.
  • Arithmetic: add / sub — cross-multiply then gcd-reduce; mul — cancel common factors first (2 gcds), then multiply; div — invert denominator, delegate to mul; all return new canonical BigRational.
  • BigRational::recip(&self) — swap num/den, fix sign; error if zero.
  • BigRational::to_integer(&self) -> BigInt — truncating division num/den.
  • BigRational::fract(&self) -> BigRational — self - self.to_integer().
  • BigRational::to_decimal(digits: usize, round: RoundMode) -> String — multiply numerator by 10^digits, divrem by denominator, insert decimal point; RoundMode: Floor, Ceil, HalfUp, HalfEven.
  • BigRational::from_f64(f: f64) -> Option<BigRational> — decompose mantissa/exponent, exact rational.

bn-int/src/ops.rs — operator impls for BigInt:

  • Implement std::ops::{Add, Sub, Mul, Div, Rem, Neg, BitAnd, BitOr, BitXor, Not} for BigInt (value and & variants).
  • From<i8/i16/i32/i64/i128/isize> — sign extraction then BigUint::from.
  • TryFrom<BigInt> for i64/i128 — error if out of range.
  • Display — minus prefix when Sign::Minus, then delegate to BigUint.

bn-uint/src/ops.rs — complete operator set for BigUint:

  • From<u8/u16/u32/u64/u128/usize>, TryFrom<BigUint> for u64/u128.
  • Display — calls bn-convert base-10 path.
  • Hash — hash the canonical limb slice only.
  • Ord — delegates to cmp_limbs (Phase 1).

bn-rational/src/ops.rs — Add/Sub/Mul/Div/Neg for BigRational; From<BigInt>, From<i64>, TryFrom<BigRational> for f64 (best-approximation via to_decimal).

bn-mod/src/lib.rs — public re-exports: montgomery_modpow(base, exp, modulus) -> BigUint, barrett_reduce(x, ctx: &Barrett) -> BigUint; hide internal REDC.

Operator macro strategy: define a impl_binop! declarative macro to reduce boilerplate across &T op &T, T op &T, &T op T, T op T.

Anchors: I10 (rational canonicality — BigRational::new must gcd-reduce and enforce den > 0 before returning); P7 (sign and magnitude are separate concerns — bn-rational/bn-int carry sign, bn-uint stays unsigned throughout ops.rs).

13.3 Deliverable

  • BigRational::new, arithmetic, to_decimal, from_f64 all callable.
  • All four type layers (BigUint, BigInt, BigRational, bn-mod public functions) expose full operator sets and Display.
  • bn-cli subcommands calc and convert compile and exercise rationals end-to-end.
  • Crate dependency graph: bn-rational → bn-int → bn-uint → bn-add/mul/div/mod/gcd/convert → bn-core.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
BigRational canon property (fuzz 10k) gcd(num,den)==1, den>0, zero has NoSign
(a/b + c/d) == (a*d + b*c)/(b*d) correctness suite 500 random cases pass
to_decimal(n, HalfEven) round-trip agrees with Python Decimal for 1000 cases
Display(BigInt) roundtrip via from_str 1000 random signed values
TryFrom<BigUint> for u64 boundary 0, u64::MAX, u64::MAX+1 (err)
All operator impls compile for &T op &T no Copy-only paths
bn-cli calc "1/3 + 1/6" prints 1/2

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose
fixtures/rational/add_cases.csv num_a,den_a,num_b,den_b,expect_num,expect_den — 200 cases
fixtures/rational/canon.csv raw_num,raw_den,canonical_num,canonical_den — 100 cases including negatives
fixtures/rational/to_decimal.csv num,den,digits,mode,expected_string — 100 cases
fixtures/rational/from_f64.csv f64_hex,expect_num,expect_den — subnormals, inf (None), NaN (None)
fixtures/api/tryconv_u64.csv bigint_hex,expect_ok,expect_val — boundary values
fixtures/api/display_roundtrip.csv 500 signed decimal strings

13.6 Pitfalls

  • Premature gcd skip in mul: skipping front-cancellation before multiplication causes quadratic blowup on rational matrix products; always cancel first.
  • Sign on zero after subtraction: a - a yields BigInt with Sign::Plus magnitude zero unless canonicalization is called; enforce I2 at every arithmetic exit.
  • Display for negative zero: BigInt with Sign::Minus and zero magnitude must not print -0; guard in fmt::Display.
  • from_f64 for f64::INFINITY / f64::NAN: must return None; forgetting the is_finite() guard produces a rational with exponent-derived garbage denominator.
  • to_decimal off-by-one in rounding: the carry after HalfUp rounding of the last digit can overflow the integer part; propagate carry through the string buffer.
  • Operator blanket impls conflict: defining impl<T: Into<BigInt>> Add<T> for BigInt conflicts with Add<BigInt> for BigInt; use concrete impls and the macro.

13.7 Performance Concerns

  • BigRational::add calls gcd twice (for front-cancellation) + once post-multiply; gcd dominates for operands > ~50 limbs — acceptable; no hot-loop rational arithmetic expected at this layer.
  • to_decimal for digits=1000 calls bn-div divide per digit group; use divide-and-conquer from bn-convert for large digit counts.
  • Operator impls must not clone unless necessary; prefer impl Add<&BigInt> for &BigInt as the primitive; owned variants delegate by reference.
  • Hash on BigUint hashes only canonical limbs; ensure no trailing-zero limbs before hash or hash is unstable.

13.8 Edge Cases

  • BigRational::new(0, 0) → DivByZero error, not panic.
  • BigRational::new(-6, -4) → 3/2 (double-negative, both cancel).
  • BigRational::recip of zero → DivByZero.
  • TryFrom<BigUint> for u64 where value is u64::MAX → Ok(u64::MAX); u64::MAX + 1 → Err.
  • from_str("-0") for BigInt → canonical zero with NoSign, not Minus.
  • Rational arithmetic with denominator 1 (integer-valued) must not produce den > 1 after reduction.
  • to_decimal(0, *) — zero digits requested: returns integer part only, no decimal point.

13.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Rational not reduced canon property test rerun gcd on canon check at debug boundary
Sign bug in signed sub Display round-trip test add sign-tracking assert at bn-int add/sub exit
TryFrom silent truncation boundary unit test Err path enforced; no silent truncation allowed
Decimal rounding diverges from reference to_decimal fixture comparison file issue; recheck carry-propagation logic
from_f64(NaN) panic property test with f64::NAN is_finite() guard, return None

13.10 Operational Notes

  • BIGNUM_LOG=rational env flag (via log crate at trace level) emits gcd call count and pre/post-reduction sizes.
  • API stability: mark pub symbols with #[doc]; internal helpers as pub(crate).
  • Minimum supported Rust version (MSRV): 1.70 (stabilized u128 carrying operations).
  • bn-rational exposes no_std-compatible path if alloc feature enabled (no std::io); to_decimal requires alloc::string::String.
  • Crate changelogs: every public API addition is a semver minor bump; breaking changes require major.

Phase 14 — hardening + conformance + fuzz + perf + packing

14.1 Objectives

  • Differential conformance: compare all operations against num-bigint, Python int, and GMP (rug crate) over random operands spanning all size classes; algorithm cross-checks confirm Karatsuba/Toom3/NTT == schoolbook for multiply and Newton == Knuth D for divrem.
  • Fuzz three high-risk surfaces: parser (from_str), divmod, and modpow.
  • Benchmark multiply and divide across size thresholds (schoolbook→Karatsuba→Toom3→NTT) and document measured crossover points.
  • Pack publishable crates (lib.rs docs, README.md, Cargo.toml metadata, feature flags, MSRV badge) and produce a final criterion benchmark suite plus a CI-runnable conformance report.

14.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Differential harness (bn-conformance/, integration test crate): generates random BigUint/BigInt pairs at limb-count classes {1,2,4,8,32,128,512,2048}; for each class runs add, sub, mul, div, rem, gcd, modpow, to_string(base10), to_string(base16), from_str and compares byte-for-byte against num-bigint (always on), optionally rug (feature rug-check) and a python3 -c subprocess (feature python-check); on failure prints operand hex, operation, our result hex, reference result hex, and size class; 1000 random pairs per class per operation by default, configurable via CONF_ROUNDS. Algorithm cross-checks (bn-mul/tests/cross.rs, bn-div/tests/cross.rs): mul_agree asserts schoolbook(a,b) == karatsuba(a,b) == toom3(a,b) == ntt(a,b) for all threshold-boundary sizes ±5 limbs; div_agree asserts knuth_d(a,b) == newton_divrem(a,b) for 500 random inputs per size class; both use a debug-only MulAlgo::Schoolbook force-flag override on BigUint mul dispatch. Fuzzing (fuzz/, cargo-fuzz + libfuzzer): fuzz_parse parses arbitrary bytes as decimal/hex, asserts no panic, and round-trips via Display on Ok; fuzz_divmod decodes two BigUints and, if the divisor is nonzero, asserts the divrem identity a == q*b + r, 0 <= r < b (I5); fuzz_modpow decodes base/exp/modulus, asserts the result lies in [0, modulus), and compares against naive schoolbook base^exp % modulus for small exponents; the seed corpus covers boundary values (0, 1, u64::MAX, 2^256-1, primes) in fuzz/corpus/. Benchmark suite (benches/): bench_mul.rs runs Criterion mul_{schoolbook,karatsuba,toom3,ntt} at 1..2048 limbs (size vs. ns/limb²); bench_div.rs runs div_{knuth,newton} at 8..1024 limbs; bench_modpow.rs runs modpow_montgomery_{1024,2048,4096}bit (target < 50 ms at 2048-bit); bench_convert.rs runs to_decimal/from_str at 100/1000/10000 digits (divide-and-conquer vs. schoolbook); Criterion config is 10 samples, 1s measurement time, --output-format=bencher for CI comparison. Crate packing (all crates): Cargo.toml metadata sets description, license = "MIT OR Apache-2.0", repository, keywords, categories, rust-version = "1.70"; feature flags:

Feature Enables
std (default) std::error::Error impls, Display, I/O
alloc no_std + alloc; no std::io
serde Serialize/Deserialize for BigUint/BigInt/BigRational
rug-check enables rug optional dep for conformance harness
python-check subprocess-based Python oracle
lib.rs carries module-level //! docs (one-paragraph description, links to referenced sources) and each crate's README.md has an install snippet, a 5-line usage example, and a docs.rs link.
CI pipeline (.github/workflows/ci.yml): cargo test --workspace --all-features; cargo test --workspace --no-default-features --features alloc; cargo bench --no-run (compile-only); cargo fuzz build (compile fuzz targets); conformance job cargo test -p bn-conformance --features rug-check (Linux only, requires libgmp-dev).

14.3 Deliverable

  • Conformance report: all 1000×8 (operand classes) × ops pass vs. num-bigint; fuzz targets compile and 60-second fuzz runs clean.
  • Criterion HTML report committed to docs/benches/; measured crossover thresholds documented in docs/thresholds.md.
  • All crates publish-ready (cargo publish --dry-run passes for each).

fixtures/conformance/gcd_cases.csv 200 pairs, expected gcd
fixtures/conformance/parse_valid.txt 500 valid decimal/hex/base-N strings + expected hex
fixtures/conformance/parse_invalid.txt 100 malformed strings: empty, overflow marker, bad char, mixed base prefix
fuzz/corpus/divmod/ boundary seed blobs: (0,1), (1,1), (2^64, 2^32), etc.
fuzz/corpus/parse/ edge string seeds: "", "0", "-0", "0x", "999…999" (1000 nines)

14.6 Pitfalls

  • Conformance divergence on negative rem: num-bigint and Python differ on sign of remainder for negative inputs; document chosen convention (I5: 0 <= r < |b|) and adjust comparison to re-derive Python's result.
  • NTT threshold not crossed in bench: if NTT is slower than Toom3 at all tested sizes, the prime set or transform length is suboptimal; instrument NttPlan selection and verify primes cover the product size.
  • Fuzz input decoding ambiguity: if fuzz bytes are decoded as little-endian limbs directly, many inputs decode to the same small value; use a length-prefixed encoding so the corpus exercises large operands.
  • rug linkage in CI: rug requires libgmp-dev; gate the conformance job on Linux and document the dependency; do not make rug-check a default feature.
  • Schoolbook force-flag only in debug: the MulAlgo override must be #[cfg(test)] or feature-gated; shipping it in release would silently degrade production performance.
  • Benchmark variance from allocation: Criterion measures wall-clock; large-multiply benchmarks are allocation-heavy; use black_box on inputs and pre-allocate result buffers in setup to isolate arithmetic from allocator noise.
  • cargo publish order dependency: crates must be published leaf-first (bn-core before bn-uint before bn-add…); document the publish order in PUBLISHING.md.

14.7 Performance Concerns

  • Conformance harness generates Vec<Limb> randomly per iteration; pre-generate a fixed seed bank and reuse to minimize allocator pressure during the comparison loop.
  • Fuzz targets run single-threaded (parse fuzzing is fast; fuzz_modpow is slow for large exp, so cap exp limb count to 4 in fuzz to keep iteration rate high); Criterion bench_mul at 2048 limbs × 10 samples (20 large multiplications) runs in a dedicated CI job to avoid shared-runner noise skewing thresholds.
  • to_decimal divide-and-conquer generates a recursion tree of BigUint allocations — profile with heaptrack at 10000-digit output to confirm O(M(n) log n) allocation profile; the serde feature serializes BigUint as a hex string (compact, human-readable), not a limb array (platform-dependent endianness).

14.8 Edge Cases

  • divrem(0, b) → (0, 0); verify in conformance fixture.
  • modpow(base, 0, m) → 1 mod m (even if base == 0); modpow(0, 0, m) → convention is 1; document.
  • modpow(base, exp, 1) → 0 always; Montgomery context with modulus=1 must not panic.
  • Parse "0x" (prefix only, no digits) → ParseError; not empty BigUint.
  • Parse "-" → ParseError; not zero.
  • Conformance with num-bigint for gcd(0, 0) → 0; ensure agreement.
  • Fuzz bytes length zero → parse returns ParseError or Ok(0) depending on implementation; pin the behavior.

14.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Conformance divergence differential harness CI job correctness of all consumers using the operation bisect by size class; compare intermediate limb buffers
Fuzz crash / panic cargo fuzz run non-zero exit any input exercising the path reproduce with minimized corpus; add regression fixture
Benchmark regression Criterion --save-baseline comparison no correctness impact; perf only check recent threshold changes; re-profile with perf stat
Publish failure (missing dep version) cargo publish --dry-run in CI release blocked fix Cargo.toml version; re-run dry-run
rug-based divergence not caught feature not enabled in CI undetected correctness bug enable rug-check in nightly CI job with libgmp-dev
Schoolbook override shipped in release audit cargo tree for test-only items production perf regression enforce #[cfg(test)] gate; clippy lint

14.10 Operational Notes

  • Conformance CI job emits a structured JSON report (conformance_report.json: timestamp, num-bigint version, op name, size class, pass/fail count); fuzz artifacts stored in fuzz/artifacts/, with committed crashing inputs becoming regression fixtures in fixtures/conformance/.
  • Threshold documentation (docs/thresholds.md): table of {schoolbook→karatsuba, karatsuba→toom3, toom3→ntt} limb-count crossovers measured on the CI runner, updated per release.
  • CHANGELOG.md at workspace root has one entry per phase completion (## [version] — Phase N complete); cargo doc --workspace --no-deps --open produces browsable API docs, and every pub item must have a doc comment before publish.
  • MSRV CI job (rustup install 1.70 && cargo +1.70 test --workspace) catches accidental MSRV regression; if any crate is used in a cryptographic context, advise consumers to enable the constant-time modpow path (P9) via montgomery_modpow_ct, documented in bn-mod crate-level docs.

Threat Model

Trust Boundaries

Per the architecture diagram: bn-cli is the external trust boundary; all other crates (bn-core through bn-rational) are internal library code. No network surface. No persistent process state. Input enters via parse() in bn-convert and via bn-cli command arguments.

[External Input: text strings, CLI args, serialized bytes]
        |
        v  TRUST BOUNDARY (bn-cli / bn-convert parse())
        |
[bn-int / bn-uint / bn-rational — internal, trusted after parse validation]
        |
        v
[bn-mul / bn-div / bn-mod / bn-gcd / bn-fftmul — pure computation, no I/O]
        |
        v
[bn-core — shared constants, zero allocations, no I/O]

Threat Table

ID Threat Surface Attack Vector Impact Mitigation Detection Recovery
T1 Integer overflow / panic in limb arithmetic bn-add, bn-mul Crafted operands triggering debug-mode overflow in u64 ops Panic → DoS; potential wrong result in release mode All limb arithmetic uses u128 (DoubleLimb); carrying ops explicit; no implicit overflow (P3, I3) Property tests; differential fuzzing; release-vs-debug comparison None needed if invariant holds; audit path on mismatch
T2 Division by zero bn-div Caller passes zero divisor UB / panic without typed error DivByZero typed error returned; never panics; zero-check precedes all div paths (I5) Unit test for zero divisor; fuzz divrem Caller handles Err(DivByZero)
T3 Memory exhaustion via huge allocation bn-mul, bn-fftmul, bn-convert Attacker-controlled exponent or operand string requesting 10^9 digits OOM crash; DoS Configurable limb-count ceiling (e.g., 1<<26 limbs ≈ 512 MiB); refuse allocation above limit; checked in bn-uint with_capacity Allocation-size log; test with 1 GB operands Return Err(Overflow) typed error; document size ceiling in API
T4 Algorithmic DoS via pathological input bn-gcd (Lehmer), bn-div (Newton) Inputs that maximize iteration count CPU exhaustion; hung process Lehmer iteration bound = O(n) limbs; Newton converges quadratically; iteration limit asserts; bench regressions gate CI Bench threshold alerts; iteration count metrics Hard iteration cap returning error
T5 Malformed string input bn-convert parse() Arbitrarily long or invalid radix strings passed to parse Panic, wrong result, DoS ParseError returned for invalid characters, empty string, overflow base; length bounded before allocation; no unwrap in parse path Fuzz bn-convert parse with arbitrary bytes; test invalid base chars Caller handles Err(ParseError)
T6 Canonicality violation (trailing zero limbs) bn-uint constructors Internally constructed BigUint with trailing zeros passed to comparison or hash Wrong equality / wrong hash bucket; silent data corruption I1 enforced by normalize() called after every construction / mutation; debug assertions check canonical form on entry to public methods Property test: normalize idempotent; assert_canonical() in debug builds Normalize before hashing; audit construction sites
T7 Sign-zero mismatch bn-int BigInt constructed with Sign::Plus/Minus on zero magnitude Wrong comparison; wrong arithmetic dispatch I2 enforced: constructor and every mutation path canonicalize sign; debug_assert in BigInt::new Fuzz BigInt construction with zero magnitudes Assert and reconstruct from magnitude
T8 NTT modulus collision / overflow bn-fftmul Operand pair whose product exceeds the CRT reconstruction range Silent wrong result — hardest to detect I8: select enough NTT primes so product(primes) > max_product; static assertion on prime set at compile time; differential test NTT vs. schoolbook (I4) CI: cross-check NTT vs. schoolbook for random large inputs Increase prime set; re-run with schoolbook if mismatch detected
T9 Timing-side-channel in modpow bn-mod, bn-gcd Observe execution time to recover private exponent bits Private key recovery in cryptographic consumers P9 / I7: constant-time modpow path uses fixed-window with dummy squarings; conditional subtraction replaced by blinded select; compile-time feature flag ct-modpow; not default for non-crypto use Benchmark variance measurement; dudect-style test in CI (Phase 14) Disable non-constant-time path for crypto callers
T10 Supply-chain (dependency confusion) Cargo.toml Malicious crate publishes under similar name Compromised library pulled into builds All crates are workspace-local; no external dependencies beyond std; Cargo.lock pinned; CI verifies no new deps added without review cargo audit; dependency diff in CI Remove rogue dep; re-pin lockfile
T11 Buffer misalignment / unsafe slice cast bn-fftmul NTT buffers Incorrect transmute of limb slices UB, memory corruption No unsafe transmute; all NTT buffers are typed Vec<u64>; if SIMD used, isolated in explicitly-unsafe block with invariant comments; reviewed in Phase 14 cargo geiger; sanitizer CI (MSAN, ASAN) Audit unsafe blocks; replace with safe indexing
T12 Operator misconfiguration of size limits bn-cli, embedding application Setting limb ceiling too high in production context OOM / DoS Document recommended limits per context; CLI validates flag ranges; embed check in library builder pattern Startup log of configured limits Reset to defaults; document safe defaults
T13 Incorrect Montgomery modulus (even) bn-mod Caller constructs Montgomery context with even modulus Wrong results silently Montgomery requires odd modulus; Montgomery::new() returns error for even m (I7) Unit tests for even-modulus rejection; fuzz modulus construction Typed error; caller corrects modulus
T14 Rational denominator zero bn-rational Construct BigRational with den=0 Panic or wrong result I10: constructor checks den != 0; returns Err(DivByZero) Unit test; fuzz rational construction Typed error

Failure Modes and Recovery

Class Symptoms Root Causes Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery Operator Actions Forensic Artifacts Long-Term Mitigation
Carry drop Wrong arithmetic result; differential test mismatch vs. Python/GMP u64 add without u128 capture; incorrect carry chain Differential fuzz; property tests (Phase 14) None — silent wrong result Reproduce input; compare with schoolbook oracle Audit bn-add / bn-mul carry logic Failing test case + minimized input P3: all limb ops use DoubleLimb; CI runs differential suite on every commit
Trailing zero limb Wrong equality (a==b fails); wrong hash; normalization not called after in-place op Forgot normalize() after growth/shrink; in-place sub leaving zeros assert_canonical() in debug; property test normalize idempotent None Run normalize(); re-test Audit every mutation site in bn-uint Failing assertion + stack trace I1 enforced: post-condition macro on every mutation
NTT wrong product Silent wrong multiply result for very large operands Prime set product too small; implementation bug in CRT recombination I4 cross-check in CI: NTT == schoolbook for random large inputs Fall back to schoolbook if mismatch flag set (dev mode) Re-run with schoolbook; verify prime bounds Check NttPlan prime product vs. max input magnitude Minimized failing input pair I8 static assertion; extend prime set before crossing size threshold
Division non-termination Process hangs on large divide; Lehmer/Newton iteration loops Off-by-one in convergence test; Newton reciprocal divergence Iteration-count hard cap; watchdog timer in CLI Hard cap returns Err Add tracing to iteration loop; compare with Knuth Algorithm D Set BIGNUM_DIV_TRACE=1 to log iteration count Iteration-count log I6 asserts; prove Newton convergence bound; fuzz with adversarial inputs
Montgomery roundtrip error Modpow gives wrong result; out(in(x)) != x mod m Incorrect n_prime computation; REDC off-by-one I7 unit tests; roundtrip property test for all operations None Verify modulus is odd; recompute n_prime Inspect Montgomery context fields Failing modpow input/output pair Automated verification of n_prime computation in context constructor
Base-conversion truncation parse(format(x)) != x; leading zeros dropped; rounding Schoolbook path stops early; D&C split point wrong I9 roundtrip fuzz; exact comparison after roundtrip None Minimize failing (value, base) pair Run bn-convert with BIGNUM_CONVERT_TRACE=1 Input value + base + output string I9 roundtrip test in every Phase 12 commit
OOM crash Process killed by OS; allocation returns null/panics No limb-count ceiling; exponent string too large; NTT buffer over-allocated Configured size limit checked before allocation Return Err(Overflow) typed error Reduce operand size; inspect caller Tune BIGNUM_MAX_LIMBS env variable Pre-crash allocation log Document and enforce size ceiling; test at boundary
Sign-zero mismatch Negative zero; wrong comparison; arithmetic dispatches wrong branch sign set to Plus/Minus with zero magnitude I2 debug_assert on every BigInt method entry normalize_sign() called in asserts Construct BigInt from magnitude: sign derived from mag Audit BigInt construction paths Failing property test input Invariant enforcement in every constructor and mutation
Rational irreducibility failure gcd(num,den) > 1 post-operation; duplicate representations GCD reduction not applied after arithmetic I10 property test: gcd(num,den)==1 after every op None Reconstruct from reduce() Audit all BigRational arithmetic operators Failing test case Call reduce() (gcd-divide) in every arithmetic result path
Constant-time violation in modpow Timing side channel leaks exponent bits Branch or memory access depending on exponent bit; non-constant-time conditional subtraction Dudect-style CI test (Phase 14); compile-time feature flag check None Replace conditional branch with blinded select Mandate ct-modpow feature in crypto consumers Timing measurements; event traces P9: dedicated constant-time code path; separate from general modpow
Panic in release build Unexpected panic propagates to caller Implicit unwrap, array out-of-bounds, debug_assert in release CI runs cargo test --release; address sanitizer None Capture panic message + backtrace Reproduce with RUST_BACKTRACE=1 Panic message + input Replace all unwrap with ?; no debug_assert in hot paths
Incorrect Toom-Cook interpolation Toom3 product wrong for operands at threshold Interpolation matrix error; intermediate overflow in integer interpolation I4 cross-check: Toom3 == schoolbook on same inputs Fall back to Karatsuba if mismatch (dev flag) Minimize failing input pair Enable BIGNUM_MUL_TRACE=1 Minimized input; MulAlgo::Toom3 selected Prove interpolation correctness; add targeted unit tests at threshold boundary

Operational Readiness

Metrics Strategy

Metric Source Type Purpose
bignum.mul.algo bn-mul dispatch Counter {algo=schoolbook karatsuba
bignum.mul.duration_ns bn-mul Histogram {algo, limb_bucket} Perf regression; SLO
bignum.div.iterations bn-div Newton/Knuth Histogram Convergence health; detect adversarial inputs
bignum.mod.redc_count bn-mod Counter Montgomery usage; modpow profiling
bignum.alloc.limb_count bn-uint Vec growth Histogram Memory pressure; size-ceiling approach
bignum.parse.errors bn-convert Counter {reason} Malformed input rate
bignum.ntt.prime_utilization bn-fftmul Gauge Headroom before CRT overflow
bignum.gcd.iterations bn-gcd Lehmer Histogram Worst-case detection

Instrumentation: feature-gated bn-metrics crate wrapping metrics facade; no overhead when disabled.

Tracing Strategy

  • Span per public operation: BigUint::mul, BigUint::divrem, modpow, from_str, to_str.
  • Attributes: limb_count_lhs, limb_count_rhs, algo_selected, iteration_count (div/gcd).
  • Exporter: OTLP to collector; BIGNUM_TRACE env variable gates emission.
  • No sensitive data (values are arithmetic operands — may be secret in crypto contexts); gate value logging behind BIGNUM_LOG_VALUES=1 (never enable in production crypto use).
  • Tracing spans nest: modpow span contains child mul spans per squaring/multiply step; iteration count aggregated at parent span.
  • Sampling: full trace for all ops > 1000 limbs; 1-in-1000 sampling for smaller ops to limit overhead.

Algorithm Threshold Observability

Threshold calibration is a first-class operational concern:

Threshold Name Default (limbs) Env Override Purpose
KARATSUBA_THRESHOLD 32 BN_KARATSUBA_THRESHOLD schoolbook → Karatsuba boundary
TOOM3_THRESHOLD 100 BN_TOOM3_THRESHOLD Karatsuba → Toom-Cook-3 boundary
NTT_THRESHOLD 3000 BN_NTT_THRESHOLD Toom3 → NTT boundary
NEWTON_DIV_THRESHOLD 200 BN_NEWTON_DIV_THRESHOLD Knuth Algorithm D → Newton reciprocal boundary
DC_CONVERT_THRESHOLD 1000 BN_DC_CONVERT_THRESHOLD schoolbook → divide-and-conquer base-convert boundary
  • Thresholds logged at startup if BIGNUM_TRACE enabled.
  • CI bench suite validates that defaults produce minimum latency across operand size sweep.
  • Threshold regression: if a new commit shifts optimal threshold > 10% from default, update the default and document in CHANGELOG.

Structured Logging

{"ts":"…","level":"WARN","msg":"limb_ceiling_approach","current":16000000,"max":16777216,"op":"mul"}
{"ts":"…","level":"ERROR","msg":"ntt_cross_check_mismatch","lhs_limbs":8192,"rhs_limbs":8192}
{"ts":"…","level":"INFO","msg":"algo_dispatch","algo":"Toom3","lhs_limbs":120,"rhs_limbs":115}

Log fields: op, algo, lhs_limbs, rhs_limbs, error_kind, iteration_count.

Dashboard Design

Panel Metric Alert Condition
Multiply throughput (limbs/s) bignum.mul.duration_ns P99 > 2x baseline
Algorithm distribution bignum.mul.algo counter NTT fraction > expected for workload
Allocation headroom bignum.alloc.limb_count P99 > 80% of ceiling
Division convergence bignum.div.iterations P99 > 3x median
Parse error rate bignum.parse.errors > 0 in production (library users)
NTT safety headroom bignum.ntt.prime_utilization > 90%

SLO Definitions

SLO Target Measurement Window
Correctness: differential match rate 100% (zero mismatches) Every CI run
add/sub ≤1000-limb latency P99 < 500 ns Bench suite
mul 1000×1000-limb P99 < 5 ms Bench suite
divrem 1000÷500-limb P99 < 10 ms Bench suite
from_str 10 000-digit decimal P99 < 2 ms Bench suite
modpow 2048-bit P99 < 50 ms Bench suite
Parse error on valid input 0 Fuzz corpus

Alerting Philosophy

  • Correctness alerts are P0: any differential mismatch triggers immediate CI block; no release proceeds.
  • Performance regressions > 20% on benchmark suite trigger P1 issue; gated on non-blocking CI lane.
  • Allocation-ceiling approach (> 80% of max) triggers WARN log; no automated response from library.
  • No paging infrastructure (library, not a service); CI failures are the primary alert surface.
  • Fuzz crash: treated as P0 (potential correctness or safety bug); immediately minimized and committed as fixture.
  • Supply-chain CVE: treated as P1 if in workspace dep; P0 if in std (Rust toolchain advisory).
  • Dudect timing failure: treated as P0 security issue when ct-modpow path is affected.

Observability Matrix by Crate

Crate Metrics emitted Trace spans Log events
bn-add None (too hot; inline) None None
bn-mul mul.algo, mul.duration_ns BigUint::mul Algo dispatch at DEBUG level
bn-fftmul ntt.prime_utilization ntt_mul child span ERROR on cross-check mismatch
bn-div div.iterations BigUint::divrem WARN on iteration cap approach
bn-mod mod.redc_count modpow INFO on Montgomery ctx creation
bn-gcd gcd.iterations gcd, ext_gcd WARN on iteration cap
bn-convert parse.errors, convert.duration_ns from_str, to_str ERROR on parse failure
bn-uint alloc.limb_count None (too fine-grained) WARN on ceiling approach

Incident Response

  1. Differential mismatch detected → pin failing input → run schoolbook oracle → identify algorithm and limb range → file P0 bug → gate all releases until resolved.
  2. OOM in production embedding → check BIGNUM_MAX_LIMBS → reduce ceiling or operand bound → redeploy.
  3. Timing side-channel report → verify with dudect harness → patch constant-time path → re-audit Phase 11 code → CVE advisory if library shipped as security-relevant component.
  4. NTT silent-wrong-result report → force schoolbook path (env flag) → verify NttPlan prime product bound → patch CRT recombination → extend prime set if needed → re-enable NTT only after CI cross-check passes.
  5. Panic in release build → capture RUST_BACKTRACE=full → minimize input → locate unwrap site → replace with typed error → regression test → patch release.
  6. Benchmark regression > 20% → cargo flamegraph on regressed benchmark → compare threshold selection vs. previous commit → check for accidental O(n^2) path → fix or re-tune threshold.

Variable Default Purpose
BIGNUM_MAX_LIMBS 16777216 (128 MiB) Hard allocation ceiling for all operations
BIGNUM_TRACE unset Enable OTLP tracing spans
BIGNUM_LOG_VALUES unset Include operand values in traces (unsafe for crypto)
BIGNUM_DIV_TRACE unset Log Newton/Knuth iteration counts to stderr
BIGNUM_MUL_TRACE unset Log MulAlgo selection and limb counts to stderr
BIGNUM_CONVERT_TRACE unset Log D&C base-convert split decisions
BN_KARATSUBA_THRESHOLD 32 Override Karatsuba dispatch threshold

Runbooks

Runbook Trigger Actions
RB-01: Differential Mismatch CI differential test fails Minimize input; run schoolbook; bisect commits; fix carry/interpolation; re-run CI
RB-02: OOM / Allocation Exhaustion Err(Overflow) returned or process OOM Reduce BIGNUM_MAX_LIMBS; audit caller operand sizes; profile allocation sites
RB-03: NTT Wrong Product Cross-check mismatch for large mul Disable NTT path; verify NttPlan prime product bound; patch CRT; re-enable after CI pass
RB-04: Timing Side-Channel External report or dudect failure Isolate to modpow ct-path; replace branch with blinded select; re-run dudect; advisory if library is shipped
RB-05: Division Non-Termination Hang detected in Newton/Lehmer Reproduce with iteration-count tracing; verify convergence bound; add hard cap; re-test
RB-06: Parse Regression Roundtrip fuzz finds mismatch Minimize failing (value, base); inspect D&C split; fix bn-convert; add regression fixture
RB-07: Benchmark Regression P99 latency > 20% baseline Profile with cargo flamegraph; identify hot path; check threshold calibration; re-benchmark
RB-08: Supply-Chain Alert cargo audit CVE Inspect affected dep; all deps should be workspace-local (no external deps by design); escalate if std dep

Backup / Artifact Management

  • No persistent state: library is stateless pure computation; no database, no files at rest.
  • Fuzz corpus (stored in fuzz/corpus/): version-controlled; add minimized failures as regression fixtures.
  • Benchmark baselines: stored as JSON artifacts in CI; compared on each run; archived per git tag.
  • Cargo.lock: pinned, committed, diffed on every PR.
  • Differential test seeds: curated set of (lhs, rhs, expected) tuples for each algorithm class; stored in tests/fixtures/; never auto-generated from the library under test.

Deployment Topology

  • Distributed as a Rust library (workspace crates on crates.io or vendored).
  • No network service; no container; no daemon.
  • Integration: embedding application links crates; no runtime configuration beyond env variables.
  • Workspace crates published independently; bn-core version anchors all transitive deps via = constraint.
  • Vendored mode: cargo vendor output committed to consuming repo; patches applied as workspace patches.

CI Pipeline Stages

Stage Tools Gate Condition
Format cargo fmt --check Any diff → fail
Lint cargo clippy -- -D warnings Any warning → fail
Unit + integration cargo test --workspace Any failure → fail
Release build cargo test --release --workspace Any failure → fail
Sanitizer ASAN + MSAN via nightly Memory error → fail
Differential Python/GMP oracle comparison (Phase 14) Any mismatch → fail
Fuzz cargo fuzz run (10 min budget per target) Crash → fail; added as fixture
Bench comparison cargo bench vs. stored baseline > 20% regression → warn/fail
Supply chain cargo audit Known CVE → fail
Coverage cargo llvm-cov < 90% line coverage → warn

Canary / Rolling Upgrade / Rollback

Topic Strategy
Canary release Publish to crates.io with pre-release version suffix (0.X.0-alpha.N); embedding application opts in explicitly
Rolling upgrade Semver-gated: patch releases are drop-in; minor releases may add API; major releases change API and ship migration guide
Rollback Pin Cargo.toml to prior version; cargo update -p bn-core --precise <prev>; fuzz corpus remains valid across versions
Migration See Compatibility Guarantees section; no binary state to migrate; re-serialize via to_str_radix(16) if limb layout changes at major boundary
Feature flags Additive features (ct-modpow, bn-metrics, bn-serde) are stable once introduced; never removed in minor releases

Compatibility Guarantees

API Stability Policy

Governed by semantic versioning (SemVer 2.0):

Change Type Version Bump Notes
New public type / trait impl minor Additive; no breaking change
New public method on existing type minor Additive
Changed method signature major Breaking; deprecation period required
Removed public item major Minimum one minor version deprecated first
Changed error variant major ParseError / DivByZero variants are stable
Changed algorithm threshold (no API change) patch Behavior-preserving; only perf changes
New MulAlgo variant minor MulAlgo is non-exhaustive; match arms must use _
Changed NttPlan fields major If NttPlan is public; prefer opaque handle

MulAlgo and NttPlan are marked #[non_exhaustive]; callers may not exhaustively match them.

Type Stability

Type Stability Notes
Limb = u64 Stable; never changes Changing would break all limb arithmetic
DoubleLimb = u128 Stable Tied to Limb
Sign enum variants Stable NoSign, Plus, Minus are SemVer-stable
BigUint fields limbs: Vec<Limb> is stable layout; field is public within crate, opaque externally Serialize via to_limbs() / from_limbs() API
BigInt fields sign, mag — opaque externally; stable via constructors
BigRational fields sign, num, den — opaque externally
DivRem fields quotient, remainder — stable
Montgomery, Barrett Opaque context types; constructor signature stable Internal fields not part of API
ParseError, DivByZero Stable error types; new variants require major bump

Wire / Serialization Compatibility

No binary wire protocol. The library is linked, not networked.

Format Stability Guarantee
Display / to_str_radix(base) Stable: produces exact canonical decimal/hex string; no trailing zeros except the value zero itself ("0"); stable across patch/minor
from_str_radix(s, base) Stable: parses canonical form produced by to_str_radix; rejects invalid input with ParseError
to_limbs() / from_limbs() Stable within major version: little-endian Vec<u64>, no trailing zeros; documents I1
JSON / binary serialization Not provided by library; embedding application owns serialization; recommended: use to_str_radix(16) for portability
Cargo.lock pinning Embedding application responsible for pinning; library guarantees behavior only for pinned versions

Schema / Type Evolution

Scenario Handling
Add new field to NttPlan Opaque type; no public field access; no consumer impact
Change MulAlgo dispatch threshold Patch-level; result is identical, timing changes only
Extend Base with new variant Minor-level; non-exhaustive enum; no existing match arm breaks
Rename internal module Not breaking if re-exported under stable public path
Change Sign::NoSign meaning Would require major + migration guide — never do this

Invariant Preservation Across Versions

I1–I10 are normative invariants of the public contract, not implementation details. Any version that violates them is a bug, not a feature change, regardless of the version component. Invariant regression is always a major bug.

Invariant Checked By Version Risk
I1 Canonical magnitude assert_canonical() debug; CI property tests Bug if violated; never intentionally relaxed
I2 Sign canonicalization Debug assert in BigInt Bug if violated
I4 Multiplication agreement CI differential suite Bug if violated; blocks release
I5 Division identity Unit tests; fuzz Bug if violated; blocks release
I9 Base-conversion roundtrip Fuzz corpus; CI Bug if violated; blocks release
I10 Rational canonicality Property tests Bug if violated

Deprecation Policy

  1. Mark item #[deprecated(since="X.Y.0", note="use Foo instead")] in minor release X.Y.
  2. Maintain deprecated item for at least one additional minor release.
  3. Remove in next major release (X+1.0.0).
  4. Deprecation is communicated in CHANGELOG.md and crate documentation.

Rolling-Upgrade Guarantees

Since the library has no persistent state, rolling upgrades reduce to: the embedding application upgrades its Cargo dependency. Cross-version state compatibility is not applicable. Serialized BigUint representations (via to_limbs() / to_str_radix()) produced by v1.x are accepted by v1.y for all y > x (minor-version compatibility). Major-version changes document migration in CHANGELOG.

Cross-Portfolio Compatibility

Consumer Portfolio Project Interface Guarantee
#87–91 (AES/SHA/RSA/Curve25519/Argon2) BigUint modpow, Montgomery reduction, modular inverse Stable public API; ct-modpow feature flag stable; timing contract documented
#92 (zk-SNARK) Modular arithmetic, GCD, BigRational Minor-version additive; no breaking signature changes without major bump
#182–184 (blockchain/EVM/Merkle-Patricia) BigUint arithmetic, base conversion Display/parse format stable; limb layout stable within major
#401 (IEEE-754 soft-float / decimal) BigRational for exact decimal conversion BigRational API stable; canonical sign and reduce semantics guaranteed

Trait and Operator Stability

Trait / Operator Stability Notes
Add, Sub, Mul, Div, Rem for BigUint/BigInt Stable in 1.x Output type fixed; panics on BigUint subtraction underflow (signed result not representable)
PartialOrd, Ord, PartialEq, Eq Stable Total order over magnitude then sign
Display, Debug Stable format Display = decimal; Debug = hex limb dump (for diagnostics, not serialization)
From<u64>, From<u128>, From<i64>, From<i128> Stable Infallible primitive conversions
TryFrom<&str> Stable; returns ParseError Alias for from_str_radix(s, 10)
Hash Stable: equal values hash identically because I1/I2 guarantee canonical form
Zero, One (num-traits if feature enabled) Stable with num-traits feature Optional; gated; version-pinned
Shl, Shr by usize Stable Returns new value; does not modify in place

Feature Flag Registry

Feature Flag Crates Affected Stability Purpose
ct-modpow bn-mod Stable once introduced; never removed Constant-time modular exponentiation path; required for P9
bn-metrics all Stable Feature-gates metrics facade; zero overhead when off
bn-serde bn-uint, bn-int, bn-rational Stable (minor) serde::Serialize / Deserialize via to_str_radix(16); format: {"hex":"..."}
bn-arbitrary bn-uint, bn-int Stable (minor) arbitrary::Arbitrary impl for fuzz targets
nightly-simd bn-fftmul Unstable; nightly-only SIMD NTT acceleration; not part of SemVer guarantees

Serialization Format Specification (serde feature)

When bn-serde enabled:

BigUint  → {"hex": "<lowercase-hex-no-leading-zeros>"}
BigInt   → {"sign": "Plus"|"Minus"|"NoSign", "hex": "<lowercase-hex>"}
BigRational → {"sign": "Plus"|"Minus"|"NoSign", "num": "<hex>", "den": "<hex>"}
  • Format version implicit in crate major version; migration guide required at major bump.
  • Consumers MUST NOT depend on the exact decimal Display string for serialization (use hex format).
  • Zero: BigUint {"hex":"0"}; BigInt {"sign":"NoSign","hex":"0"}.

MSRV (Minimum Supported Rust Version) Policy

  • MSRV: stable Rust, pinned to a specific version in Cargo.toml (rust-version field).
  • MSRV bumps require a minor version increment (not patch).
  • MSRV is tested in CI on every commit by running against the pinned toolchain.
  • Nightly-only features (nightly-simd) are gated behind a feature flag and excluded from MSRV guarantees.

Numeric Semantics Contract

Callers depending on numeric semantics across versions:

Operation Guaranteed Behavior Cross-version Stability
divrem(a, b) a = q*b + r; 0 <= r < b; truncated toward zero Stable I5 — never changes
BigInt % operator Same sign as dividend (Rust % convention) Stable; documented
modpow(b, e, m) Result in [0, m); m must be odd for Montgomery path Stable I7
gcd(a, b) Non-negative; gcd(0,0)=0 Stable
inv_mod(a, m) Returns x s.t. a*x ≡ 1 (mod m); error if gcd(a,m) != 1 Stable
from_str_radix("0", 10) Returns zero with I1/I2 canonical form Stable
Shift by zero x << 0 == x; no allocation Stable

Testing Strategy

Unit Testing

Layer Test Target Technique
bn-core Limb constants, DoubleLimb promotion, Sign canonicalization Assert exact values; exhaustive Sign enum
bn-uint Normalization idempotency, comparison total order Property: normalize(normalize(x))==normalize(x)
bn-add carry conservation: len(a+b)<=max(len_a,len_b)+1 Random u64 pairs, verify against u128 reference
bn-add add-mul-1 compare a+=b*c vs schoolbook for all limb boundary values
bn-mul Schoolbook correctness Diff against u128 for 1–3 limb inputs; exact match
bn-div DivRem contract a==q*b+r, 0<=r<b Exhaustive small, random large
bn-mod Barrett/Montgomery roundtrip, range [0,m) Random (a,m) pairs; result==naive reduce
bn-gcd gcd(a,b)lcm(a,b)==ab Property over random pairs
bn-convert roundtrip: parse(format(x,base))==x All bases 2..36, random magnitudes
bn-rational Canonical: gcd(num,den)==1, den>0, sign on num Constructor + arithmetic output

Integration Testing

  • Cross-crate flows: bn-cli factorial N → verify output matches known factorial values up to 1000!.
  • modpow(base, exp, mod) end-to-end: bn-int through bn-mod, results checked against Python int pow().
  • BigRational arithmetic chains: (a/b)+(c/d)-(e/f) == reduced reference.
  • Parse → arithmetic → format in base 10/16/2 for 100-digit and 10 000-digit operands.
  • bn-cli calc subcommand: exercise every operator via subprocess, compare stdout to Python.

Differential / Conformance Testing

Subject algorithm Oracle Method
Karatsuba product Schoolbook product (Phase 4) I4: diff all limb-count classes 0–4000
Toom-Cook-3 product Schoolbook product I4: operands near and above Toom threshold
NTT product Schoolbook product I8: random pairs in NTT size class; bit-exact match
Newton divrem Knuth Algorithm D I6: random (a,b) pairs, all size classes
Barrett reduction Naive a mod m I7: random (a,m); check result and roundtrip
Montgomery REDC Naive a mod m I7: random (a,m) including m=1 edge
Lehmer GCD Binary GCD Cross-check for 10 000 random pairs
Extended GCD Verify: as+bt==gcd(a,b) Identity check, not oracle comparison
Base-conversion Python int(str(x), base) I9: random large integers, bases 2,10,16
BigRational ops Python fractions.Fraction 10 000 random rational arithmetic expressions

Reference environment: Python 3.12 subprocess or pre-generated test vectors stored in tests/vectors/.

Property-Based Testing (proptest)

  • BigUint from arbitrary limb vecs → normalize → re-normalize: idempotent (I1).
  • Commutativity: a+b==b+a, ab==ba over all size classes.
  • Associativity: (a+b)+c==a+(b+c), (ab)c==a(bc).
  • Distributivity: a*(b+c)==ab+ac.
  • DivRem identity for all (a,b) b!=0 (I5).
  • GCD: gcd(a,b) divides both a and b; result is non-negative.
  • Modular exp: modpow(a,p-1,p)==1 for prime p (Fermat little theorem).
  • Rational arithmetic: a/b + c/d == (ad+bc)/(b*d) (before reduction).
  • Strategy sizes: 0 limbs, 1, 2, 3, n<16, n<256, n<4096 — proptest parameter sets.

Fuzzing

Fuzz target Crate Seed corpus
fuzz_parse bn-convert All base-10 edge strings, empty, overflow, leading zeros
fuzz_divrem bn-div Pairs: b=0 (expect DivByZero), b=1, a<b, a==b, very large
fuzz_modpow bn-mod (base,exp=0,mod=1), exp=1, large exp with Montgomery
fuzz_from_str_roundtrip bn-convert Random byte sequences; must not panic, only ParseError
fuzz_mul_commute bn-mul Assert ab==ba at all algorithm tiers

Harness: cargo-fuzz (libFuzzer); run in CI on each PR for 60 s; nightly runs for 8 h. Corpus artifacts committed under fuzz/corpus/.

Chaos and Fault Injection

  • Out-of-memory simulation: wrap Vec::push in a test allocator that fails at configurable allocation count N; verify no UB, only panic or error return.
  • Integer overflow probes: inputs at u64::MAX, u128::MAX, zero — no silent truncation.
  • Stack-depth probes: recursive Toom-Cook and divide-and-conquer base-conversion at maximum recursion depth; verify no stack overflow.

Deterministic Replay

  • All property and fuzz failures are reproducible by seed; proptest seeds committed in tests/seeds/.
  • CI logs emit the exact proptest seed on failure.

Soak Testing

  • 24-hour soak: continuous random arithmetic with self-consistency checks (DivRem identity, GCD divisibility, multiplication commutativity) — catches rare carry-propagation edge cases.
  • Memory stability: soak must not show monotonic RSS growth (detects Vec leak or buffer reuse bug).

Performance Regression

  • Criterion benchmarks for: multiply at 2, 16, 128, 1024, 4096, 16384-limb operands; divrem at same sizes; modpow 2048-bit, 4096-bit.
  • CI records baseline on main; PR blocks if any benchmark regresses >5% (p-value < 0.05 with Criterion's Welch test).
  • Saved baselines under benches/baselines/; diff plotted in CI artifact HTML.

Conformance Validation

  • Test vectors: NIST / known RSA/DSA operands for modpow correctness.
  • Factorial: first 500 factorials from a known-good table.
  • Fibonacci: F(1000000) compared to Python mpmath output.

Regression

  • Every reported bug produces a minimized non-property regression test added to tests/regression/ named by issue number.

CI/CD Topology

PR open
  └─ lint (clippy --deny warnings, rustfmt --check)
  └─ test matrix (stable, beta, MSRV=1.75 × linux/mac/win)
  └─ fuzz (60 s per target, libFuzzer)
  └─ bench regression (Criterion vs stored baseline)
  └─ miri (UB/unsafe check on core arithmetic kernels)
  └─ cargo-deny (license + advisory audit)
  └─ SBOM generation (cargo-cyclonedx)

main merge
  └─ full bench suite → update baseline artifact
  └─ nightly fuzz (8 h, parallel workers)
  └─ publish-dry-run for all crates

tag vX.Y.Z
  └─ cargo publish (bn-core → bn-uint → bn-add → bn-mul → bn-fftmul →
                    bn-div → bn-mod → bn-gcd → bn-int → bn-convert →
                    bn-rational → bn-cli)  [topological order]
  └─ artifact signing (cargo-dist + sigstore cosign)
  └─ SBOM attached to GitHub release
  └─ changelog generated from conventional commits

Dependency Auditing and Supply-Chain

  • cargo-deny: blocks new crates with GPL-incompatible licenses, RUSTSEC advisories, duplicate versions of num-traits.
  • cargo-audit: run on each PR and nightly; any RUSTSEC advisory fails CI.
  • Dependency count goal: zero runtime deps for bn-core through bn-gcd; optional features gate serde, num-traits impls.
  • cargo-vet: trust policy for all transitive deps; audit records stored in supply-chain/.

Artifact Signing and Provenance

Artifact Signing method

SBOM CycloneDX JSON, signed and attached to release
Provenance SLSA Level 2 via slsa-github-generator

Version Stamping

  • Crate versions follow SemVer strictly; bn-core version is the root version; all crates in the workspace share the same version number.
  • build.rs in bn-cli embeds GIT_SHA and build date into --version output.
  • API-breaking changes increment major version; MSRV bumps are major.
